Retention:

- [x] `last_referenced_at` maintenance on every unreference event and on creation.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `d4b78c8`. `retention/References` (`touch(c, itemId, now)`, `touchPlaylist(c, playlistId, now)`; `UPDATE … WHERE last_referenced_at < now`, so never backwards) is now the only writer of `last_referenced_at` after creation, called inside the transaction that makes the change; it replaces five private copies in `Library`, `Playlists`, `Shares`, `Uploads` and `YoutubeImports`. Covered events: creation (the insert in `Uploads`/`YoutubeImports`), library entry add/remove, share, playlist entry add/remove, playlist delete (every item in it, before the entries go), dedupe/re-ingest/import of an existing item. **Added:** a running ingest job counts as a reference (`libraries.md#retention`), so a job that finishes or fails, of any kind, also bumps it (`IngestJobs.complete`/`fail`, and `recoverInterrupted` when it gives up; a requeued job still references the item, so no bump). Users are never hard-deleted, so the `ON DELETE CASCADE`s on library entries/playlists aren't an unreference path today. Queue entries finishing (M3) should call `References.touch` (noted for the "Retention integration for queue entry states" step). Tests: `ReferencesContract` (SQLite + PostgreSQL: forward only, playlist touches every item, others untouched), `IngestJobsContract` asserts the bump on complete/fail/give-up and none on requeue. `mvn verify` passes (758 unit + 225 integration).
- [x] Hourly sweeper: select, re-check in a transaction, delete files (and overlay caches), mark `purged`; `retention.unreferencedDays = 0` disables.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `3ea5b30` (no schema change). `retention/RetentionSweeper` (started in `Main` after the services, closed first in the shutdown hook): a single-thread scheduler on a virtual thread, first sweep 1 min after start, then every hour (fixed delay); a failing sweep is logged and the schedule continues. Each sweep reads `retention.unreferencedDays` (`0` → nothing), selects in id-ordered pages of 500 (cursor `id > last`) the items with `status='ready'`, `last_referenced_at < now − days` (strictly older) and no library entry, playlist entry, `queued`/`suggested`/`playing` queue entry or `running` ingest job, and purges each through `Items.purge` with a `PurgeCheck` that re-runs the same predicate for that item after the row lock (`FOR UPDATE` on PostgreSQL) and rolls back if it no longer holds (counted as skipped; so is an `item_busy`). So the purge order is the manual purge's (mark + move aside in the transaction, delete after the commit; deviation already noted above). **Decisions:** queued ingest jobs don't protect an item (they are cancelled like in any purge; only a running job counts, per `libraries.md`); each purge writes an `item.purged` audit row with no actor/IP and details `{previousStatus, reason:"retention", unreferencedDays}`; a per-item failure (file or DB error) is logged and the sweep goes on; accepted race (doc'd in the class and `libraries.md`): an add that read the item `ready` just before the sweep can commit its entry just after, which then shows "media expired". **Overlay caches:** none exist until M5; the sweeper goes through `Items.purge`, whose `TODO(M5)` covers both purge paths. Tests: `RetentionSweeperContract` (5, `SqliteRetentionSweeperTest` + `PostgresRetentionSweeperIT`: which items go (boundary at exactly the cutoff kept, each reference kind protects, `played` queue entry and queued job don't, pending/failed untouched, files deleted, row/metadata kept, audit rows), `0` disables and the setting is re-read, the in-transaction re-check skips items referenced/touched/queued after the selection, paging, start/close), `DtoIpLeakTest` classifies `RetentionSweeper$Result` as internal. `libraries.md` updated. `mvn verify` passes (763 unit + 230 integration). Jar smoke test: starts, `/api/me` 200, stops cleanly.

UI:

- [x] Library, item page (direct link), playlists, upload (chunked, resumable) and YouTube import, job status.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `4747ea8` (no API or schema change). **Pages:** `/library/` (`web/src/library.ts`): the caller's library with search (`q`), kind filter and "Load more", per row title link to the item page, kind, length, status badge (pending/processing items poll their job every 2 s and show its progress), how it was added (incl. "Shared by"), "Add to playlist…" picker and Remove; unseen received shares at the top with "Mark all as seen"; an **Add media** panel (`addmedia.ts`, shown per `library.upload` / `library.youtube` from `/api/me`): multi-file picker and drag & drop, "Add uploads to my library" checkbox, and a YouTube link form with "Audio only" (`presentAsAudio`); each upload/import gets an activity row that follows the item's job until ready/failed. `/items/{id}` (**new route** `GET /items/{id}` in `Main.router`, served by the new `StaticFiles.page("item.html")`: same headers, GET/HEAD only, 404 if missing; the page reads the id from the path): open to anyone with the link (no `requireUser`); cover (hidden if none), title/artist/album/length/kind/source/added, status, failure reason, "media expired" text, a player once ready (`audio.m4a`, or `mezz.mp4` for a video not presented as audio; previews only, M3 does the real player with originals), job panel (summary, progress, times; log for those who may edit, open by default when failed), and for users: add to / remove from library ("Fetch again…" for a purged YouTube item), playlist picker, share form, edit form (`canEdit`; title/artist/album/presentAsAudio), Purge (`canPurge`), Copy link. The edit/share forms live outside the polled part so typing isn't lost. `/playlists/` (`playlists.ts`): list (newest change first, Load more) and create; `/playlists/?id=…`: entries with ↑/↓ (PATCH with `beforeEntryId`), Remove, total length, edit name/description, delete. Nav (`session.ts`) gains Library and Playlists for users; front page links them. **Uploads** (`uploads.ts`): `POST /api/uploads`, then `PUT` chunks of the server's `chunkSize` with `Content-Range` through XHR (progress events), `complete`; pause/resume/cancel; network errors, 408 and 5xx retried with 1/2/5/10/30 s backoff; a `409` re-reads the offset; the upload id is kept in localStorage under size+mtime+name (7 days), so after a reload or crash picking the same file again resumes from `GET /api/uploads/{id}`'s `receivedBytes` (the page lists remembered unfinished uploads); forgotten on completion or on a refusal (404/413/422…), kept on 401/403. No upload-cancel endpoint exists, so a cancelled upload is only forgotten client-side (the server's tmp cleanup removes it). Safari 15 baseline kept (no `requestSubmit`, `.at`, etc.). **Not done here:** browsing other users' libraries (`library.viewAll`) and the sent-shares list have no UI yet. Tests: `StaticFilesTest` +1 (`page()`: headers, HEAD, 304, PUT/DELETE 405 and file untouched, missing/`..`/hidden → 404), `MainRouterTest` +1 (`/items/{id}` serves the page, `/library/` and `/playlists/` load), `WebBuildTest` +1 (pages and scripts packaged, login gate on library/playlists but not the item page, API paths), `PermissionMatrixTest` and `DtoIpLeakTest` know the new route. `mvn verify` passes (765 unit + 230 integration). **Browser smoke test** (headless Chrome over CDP against the shaded jar, SQLite): MP3 upload → Ready and listed by its tag title; a 13 MB WAV uploaded at a throttled 4 MB/s, paused after 1 MiB, page reloaded (reminder shown), same file picked again → "(continuing)" → Ready with the full 70 s; localStorage cleared after; item page title/player/job log/edit; playlist create, add two items from the library, move down persisted; search; YouTube `youtu.be/jNQXAC9IVRw` imported to Ready; anonymous visitor sees the item page without user actions, unknown id shows Not found, `/library/` redirects to login; no page exceptions. Not yet looked at on Safari/iOS or Firefox (the M2 "Done when" and later manual browser steps can cover it).

## M3 — Rooms and synchronised web playback

Server:

- [x] Room CRUD (`/api/rooms…`), slugs, visibility, room members and roles, `rooms.create`, `rooms.moderateAny`, ownership transfer.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `b1e8f57`, in `rooms/`: `Rooms` (records only; live state comes with the executor step) and `RoomsApi`. Routes: `GET/POST /api/rooms`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/rooms/{room}`, `GET /api/rooms/{room}/members`, `PUT/DELETE /api/rooms/{room}/members/{userId}`; `{room}` is the id or the slug everywhere. Defaults: `unlisted`, `vote`, `settings_json` `{}` (defaults come with the room-settings step), `playback_state` `idle`. Slugs 3–40 of `a-z0-9-`, letter/digit at both ends, never UUID-shaped, lower-cased on input; derived from the name (NFKD, marks dropped, `-2`, `-3`, … then a random suffix; `room` if nothing usable is left); slug choices are serialised by a PostgreSQL advisory xact lock, with unique violations as a fallback (`409 slug_taken`). At most 100 owned rooms (`409 too_many_rooms`). Visibility: public listed for all; unlisted/private listed only for owner/moderators/members; private is `404 room_not_found` to everyone else (moderateAny may open it but doesn't get it listed); banned users get `403 banned_from_room` (`404` for private) and never see it listed. moderateAny = moderator everywhere, also overriding a ban. PATCH: owner only (not moderateAny). DELETE: owner or moderateAny; touches `last_referenced_at` of queued/suggested/playing items. Roles: owner sets any role for anyone but self; moderators only member/banned for non-owner, non-moderator users; nobody changes their own role (`409 cannot_change_own_role`). **Ownership transfer = `PUT …/members/{userId}` with `role:"owner"`** (owner only, enabled target, old owner becomes moderator; `rooms.owner_id` and the `owner` member row move together). DELETE member also lifts bans and is how users leave (owner can't: `409 owner_cannot_leave`; banned users can't remove their own ban). Audited `room.created/updated/deleted/member_role/member_removed/owner_transferred`. `UploadMetadata.clean` made public for room names. Tests: `RoomsContract` (13, SQLite + PostgreSQL), `RoomsApiTest`, new rows in `PermissionMatrixTest`, rooms DTOs classified/produced/swept in `DtoIpLeakTest`. `mvn verify` passes (784 unit + 243 integration). Follow-ups for later steps: PATCH of `controlMode`/`visibility` and DELETE must also go through the room executor once it exists; room-dir/overlay cleanup on delete is M5.
- [x] `RoomState` + single-threaded per-room executor: mutate → persist → bump version → broadcast → notify packager hook (no-op until M4).
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `2cc6bff`, in `rooms/`: `RoomRegistry` (one `RoomExecutor` per room, loaded from the `rooms` row on first use, kept until deleted or shutdown; `call(roomId, mutation)` retries on a closed executor so a deleted room ends in `404 room_not_found`; `503 shutting_down` after close; closed in Main's shutdown hook before the database). `RoomExecutor`: a `ScheduledThreadPoolExecutor(1)` on a virtual thread; `submit`/`call` (refused with `IllegalStateException` on its own thread, it would deadlock) and `schedule` (for the advance task). A `RoomMutation` gets a `RoomTx`: `state()`, `now()`, `persist(SqlWork)`, `event(RoomEvent)`, `timeline(Timeline)` (sets it and adds `TimelineChanged`). Mutations check first, change after. If a mutation registered writes or events: its writes and the room row update (`version`+1, `playback_state`, `current_entry_id`, anchors) run in **one transaction**, then the in-memory version is bumped, then `RoomListener.onChange(state, RoomChange)` (on the executor thread, must not block), then `PackagerHook.roomChanged(change)` (`PackagerHook.NONE` until M4; never feeds back). Read-only mutations: nothing persisted, no bump, no broadcast. A non-`HttpError` failure, a failure after registering writes/events, or a failed transaction reloads the state from the DB (no broadcast, version unchanged); a row gone behind its back closes the executor (404). Closing: `onClosed(roomId, deleted)` to listeners and the hook, queued work fails with `RoomClosedException`, delayed tasks dropped. `RoomState` holds the room record, raw `settings_json`, the immutable `Timeline` (`PlaybackState` IDLE/PLAYING/PAUSED; an unknown stored state loads as paused with a warning; switches are exhaustive, no default) and `version`; queue/votes/presence/typed settings come with their steps. Follow-ups from the CRUD step done: `PATCH /api/rooms/{room}`, `PUT …/members/{userId}` (ownership transfer) and `DELETE /api/rooms/{room}` now run on the room's executor (`RoomEvent.RoomUpdated` only when the record actually changed; delete closes the executor); `Rooms.idOf`/`Rooms.record` added. Tests: `RoomExecutorContract` (11, SQLite + PostgreSQL: order mutate→persist→bump→listener→packager, shared transaction, read-only, 200 concurrent mutations strictly serial with consecutive versions, refusal, rollback on failed write and on a bug, call-on-own-thread, scheduling/cancel, restart reload, delete with queued work, delete behind its back), `RoomsApiTest.changesGoThroughTheRoomExecutor`; new records classified INTERNAL in `DtoIpLeakTest`. `mvn verify` passes (796 unit + 254 integration).
- [x] Timeline: extensible `PlaybackState` (no "else = playing"), position math, re-anchoring, advance task rescheduled on every re-anchor, skipping not-ready entries, resume after restart.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `abd510b`, in `rooms/`. `Timeline`: `positionAt(now)` (playing: `anchorPos + (now − anchorServer)`, paused: frozen, idle: 0), `positionAt(now, duration)` clamped, `endsAtServerMs(duration)` (only while playing), `playing/paused(entry, now, pos)` re-anchor factories; the constructor enforces idle ⇔ no entry. Every `PlaybackState` switch is exhaustive with no `default`. New `QueueEntry` record (row + item status/duration; `ready()` = status `ready` with a positive duration) and `QueueEntry.State` (queued/suggested/playing/played/skipped/removed). `RoomState` now holds `current()` (the timeline's entry) and `queue()` (the `queued` entries); `RoomStore.load` reads them with their items (unknown entry states skipped with a warning); setting a timeline directly keeps `current()` on its entry. `Playback` (primitives, no permission checks; the queue-ops step adds those): `advance(tx, PLAYED|SKIPPED)` ends the current entry and starts the next eligible one at 0 (`played_at` = start time, entry state `playing`) or goes idle; `startIfIdle`, `play` (paused → playing; idle → start next), `pause`, `seek` (clamped to the entry; a seek to the end of a playing entry advances at once); `resume` (start-up). Item status/duration are re-read from the DB before choosing (ingest finishes on other threads), so a not-ready entry stays `queued`, is skipped over, and is eligible at the next advance once ready. **Play order for now:** pinned first, then `position`, `added_at`, id (`Playback.PLAY_ORDER`); the control-mode step refines it (votes in vote mode). Events: new `RoomEvent.EntryStateChanged(entryId, state)`. **Advance task:** `RoomExecutor` keeps one, rescheduled after every committed change with `TimelineChanged`, after a reload, and when a mutation calls `RoomTx.rescheduleAdvance()` (resume; a task that ran early); it runs `Playback.advanceIfDue` on the executor. **Restart:** `RoomRegistry` runs `Playback.resume` as the first task of every executor it creates, and `Main` calls `RoomRegistry.resumePlaying()` at start (loads every `playing` room). A playing room keeps its anchor (nothing written) if its computed position is inside the entry, restarts the entry at 0 (re-anchored now) if the server was down past its end, and skips (as `skipped`) a current entry whose item is no longer `ready`; paused/idle rooms aren't loaded and stay put; an unknown stored state loads frozen as paused (no advance task). Tests: `TimelineTest` (6), `PlaybackContract` (10, SQLite + PostgreSQL: advance at the end skipping processing/failed entries, an entry becoming ready mid-session, idle at the end, nothing-ready stays idle, play/pause/seek re-anchoring with the advance-task delay checked after each, seek past the end, skip, play order, resume within / past the remaining time, resume skipping a purged current item, paused rooms not resumed, unknown state frozen). `mvn verify` passes (812 unit + 264 integration). Follow-ups: when an item becomes ready, idle rooms with an entry for it should start it (`Playback.startIfIdle`); there is no ingest → rooms notification yet, so wire it with the queue-ops / live current-next step (adding to an idle queue should call `startIfIdle` too).
- [x] Control modes `owner` / `vote` / `open`: queue ordering, suggestions accept/reject, votes (+ optional downvotes), pin, vote-skip threshold, permissions per op.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `7fdd0f3`, in `rooms/`. `RoomActor` (userId or guestId, effective room role, `canRequest`, `canVote`; `user(id, role)`: rights unless banned; `guest(id, canRequest, canVote)`: the caller passes server AND room capabilities, wired by the guests-in-rooms step; `ANONYMOUS` → `401 identity_required`). `ControlPolicy` (pure checks, table in its Javadoc): `add` → `QUEUE`/`SUGGESTION` (owner/mods always queue; others need request rights, `403 cannot_request`, and suggest in `owner` mode); `addPlaylist` and `suggest.accept/reject` mods only in every mode; `remove` mods, the requester while queued/suggested, and in `open` anyone with request rights; `move` mods in `owner`, request rights in `open`, `409 wrong_control_mode` in `vote`; `pin` vote mode, mods; `vote` vote mode, vote rights (`403 cannot_vote`), `±1/0` (`400 invalid_vote`), `-1` only with `allowDownvotes` (`403 downvotes_disabled`); **play/pause/seek/next/previous** = "who can skip": mods, plus anyone with request rights in `open`; `voteSkip` vote mode, vote rights, `409 vote_skip_off`; `capabilities()` for the state's `you`. `ControlSettings` reads `allowDownvotes` / `voteSkipThreshold` (number in (0,1] or `"off"`) leniently from `settings_json` (the room-settings step replaces this); `skipVotesNeeded(present)` = ceil(threshold × max(present, 1)), at least 1. **Play order** (`Playback.playOrder`): `owner`/`open` manual by position (pins ignored); `vote`: pinned first (in pin order, a new pin goes below older pins via position = end), then vote total descending, ties by `added_at`. `RoomState` now also holds suggestions, votes (entry → voter key → ±1), skip votes on the current entry (cleared when the current entry changes) and `presentIdentified` (set by the WebSocket layer later; 0 counts as 1); `RoomStore.load` reads them. `QueueControl`: `vote` (delete + insert, same value again is a no-op), `pin(entryId, pinned)` (also unpin), `accept` (→ queued at the end, `startIfIdle`), `reject` (→ `removed`, item `last_referenced_at` touched), `voteSkip` (one per voter, skips with `Playback.advance(SKIPPED)` at the threshold, `409 nothing_playing`), `checkVoteSkip` (for presence drops), `controlModeChanging` (called from `RoomsApi` PATCH before the new mode is taken over: positions rewritten to the old play order 1..n and pins cleared, `QueueReordered`). New events `VotesChanged`, `EntryPinned`, `QueueReordered`, `SkipVotesChanged`. Decisions: votes and vote-skip exist only in `vote` mode; suggestions left over after leaving `owner` mode stay and can still be accepted/rejected by mods in any mode. Tests: `ControlPolicyTest` (every op × mode × owner/mod/member/no-role/banned/guest/guest-without-rights/anonymous, settings parsing, per-mode order), `QueueControlContract` (7, SQLite + PostgreSQL). `mvn verify` passes (829 unit + 271 integration). Not here (queue-ops step): `add`/`addPlaylist`/`remove`/`move`/play controls over the WebSocket, using these checks.
- [x] Queue ops: add (item / uploadId / youtubeUrl), addPlaylist (+ shuffle), remove, move, pin, vote, voteSkip, play, pause, seek, next, previous; `requestsPerActor`, `noRepeatWindow`.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `d38746c`, in `rooms/`. `QueueOps` (executor primitives, each checks `ControlPolicy` first): `checkAdd` (identity, request rights, `requestsPerActor` → `409 request_limit`, counting the actor's queued **and** suggested entries, not the current one), `add(itemId)` (also `404 item_not_found`, `409 item_failed` / `media_expired`, `noRepeatWindow` → `409 recently_played`: the item started playing in the last N entries by `played_at`, current included; then a new entry at the end, `queued` or `suggested` per `ControlPolicy.add`, item `last_referenced_at` = now, `startIfIdle`), `addPlaylist(itemIds)` (mods only, so no limits; unplayable items left out and counted), `remove` (queued/suggested only, the current entry is `404 entry_not_found`: use `next`; → `removed`, item touched), `move(entryId, beforeEntryId|null)` (`409 before_entry_not_found`; midpoint between the neighbours in manual order, renumbering the queue 1..n when there is no gap left), `play`, `pause`, `seek` (`400 invalid_position`, `409 nothing_playing` when idle), `next` (skip → `skipped`; `409 nothing_playing` when idle), `previous`. **Decisions:** owner and moderators are exempt from `noRepeatWindow` as well as `requestsPerActor` (both limit *requests*); settings read leniently by `ControlSettings` (`requestsPerActor` 1..1000, default 3; `noRepeatWindow` 0..1000, default 10, 0 = off) until the room-settings step. **`previous`:** more than 3 s into the entry restarts it; otherwise the entry that played or was skipped last (by `played_at`) plays again from 0 (same row, back to `playing`; its votes/skip votes deleted) and the current entry goes back to the front of the queue (`queued`, before the first entry; in vote mode as the top pin); a paused room stays paused, an idle room replays the last entry, nothing to go back to = restart. `RoomQueue` (service for the WebSocket step, over `RoomRegistry`): runs `checkAdd` on the executor first, then resolves the source outside it: `itemId` (purged/failed YouTube item → `AddMediaPolicy.requireYoutube` + `YoutubeImports.useExisting`), `uploadId` (`Uploads.get` for the actor: `404 upload_not_found`, `409 upload_incomplete` until completed), `youtubeUrl` (`requireYoutube`, then `importUrl` with `addToLibrary=false`, so a room request doesn't fill the requester's library); `400 invalid_source` unless exactly one is given; `addPlaylist(playlistId, shuffle)` reads one of the caller's own playlists (`404 playlist_not_found`), refetches purged/failed YouTube items when allowed, shuffles with an injected `Random`; thin wrappers for remove/move/pin/vote/voteSkip/accept/reject/play/pause/seek/next/previous. New events `EntryAdded`, `EntryMoved`. `RoomQueue` isn't wired into `Main` yet (its only caller is the WebSocket step). Tests: `QueueOpsContract` (10, SQLite + PostgreSQL), `ControlPolicyTest.controlSettings` extended, new records classified INTERNAL in `DtoIpLeakTest`. `mvn verify` passes (839 unit + 281 integration). Not here: request-scope check on `add` by item id (request-scope step), `room_requests` rows (request-history step; `RoomQueue.Source.via()` is ready for it), guest room capabilities in `RoomActor` (guests-in-rooms step), starting idle rooms when an item becomes ready (still open from the timeline step; live current/next step).
- [x] Room settings (`settings_json`) with defaults from `docs/rooms.md#room-settings-settings_json`.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `cd32fb6`, in `rooms/`. `RoomSettings` (immutable; one definition table of 20 settings with default and validator; full list now in `docs/rooms.md`): nested objects on the wire and in storage (`{"guests":{"canVote":true}}`, dotted keys refused); `parse` reads `settings_json` leniently (unknown/invalid → default), `patch(JsonObject)` is a strict merge patch (`null` resets a setting or a whole group; `400 unknown_setting` / `invalid_setting` with the dotted name in the message), `toJson()` = every setting (the room view), `toStoredJson()` = only values that differ from the defaults (new rooms keep `{}`), `changedFrom`, typed accessors (`requestScope()`, `guestsCan*()`, `overlay*()`, `rtmpPlaybackUrl()`, `tsJoinLatest()`, `estimatedDelayMs*()`, `control()`). `ControlSettings` is now a part of it (`ControlSettings.parse` delegates). **API:** `PATCH /api/rooms/{room}` takes `settings` (owner only, all or nothing together with the other fields; `400 invalid_settings` if not an object; audit `room.updated` lists the changed setting names, no values); the room object (`GET`, list, `POST`, `PATCH`) has `settings` with every value; `POST` does not take `settings`. **Live state:** `RoomState.settings()`; the PATCH runs on the room executor and `syncRecord` takes changed settings over with a new `RoomEvent.SettingsChanged(changed names)` (version bump), then `QueueControl.checkVoteSkip` (a lowered threshold may already be reached). **Decisions:** `overlay.margin` is % of the frame height (default 3, 0–20), `overlay.heightPct` 5–50; `outputs.rtmp.playbackUrl` is `""` for none (so the wire form never drops it), http(s)/rtmp(s)/rtsp(t) with a host, ≤ 2048 chars; `outputs.ts.join` `next`/`latest` (the `join=latest` room setting from program-output.md); `estimatedDelayMs.{rtmp,ts,hls}` 0–120000; `gapMs` 0–10000. Output on/off is **not** a room setting: it stays `stream_outputs.enabled` (M4 outputs API), one source of truth. Server switches are not enforced when saving: effective = server AND room where used (guests step, M4, M5); the server-disabled greying is for the owner-view UI step, which needs a way to read the server switches (not added here). Existing downvotes stay when `allowDownvotes` is turned off. Tests: `RoomSettingsTest` (defaults, wire form, lenient read, merge/reset, strictness), `RoomsApiTest.settings` (patch, wire, storage form, events/version, no-op patch, reset, all-or-nothing, moderators refused), `DtoIpLeakTest` classifications. `mvn verify` passes (845 unit + 281 integration).
- [x] Guests in rooms: effective capability = server AND room; guest bans (IP-mode warning without IP); presence counts (identified vs anonymous).
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `76bd3c4`, in `rooms/`. `RoomGuests` (implements `RoomGuestRules`, wired into `AddMediaPolicy` in `Main` instead of `NO_ROOMS`): `Capabilities(canVote, canRequestLibrary, canUpload, canYoutube)`; `effective(guestsEnabled, server, room)` = each server AND room setting; server half from `Settings` (`guests.canYoutube` also needs `ytdlp.enabled`); `guests.enabled` off or a guest banned server-wide → none; an anonymous visitor gets what it would get as a guest. `roomActor(actor, room)` builds the `RoomActor` (after `Rooms.access`): users with their room role plus `library.upload` / `library.youtube`+`ytdlp.enabled`, guests with effective capabilities, `ANONYMOUS`. `require(room, guest, UPLOAD|YOUTUBE)` = room half (`404 room_not_found` incl. private rooms, `403 banned_from_room`, `403 guest_uploads_disabled` / `guest_youtube_disabled`). **`RoomActor` changed:** `canRequest` split into `canRequestLibrary`, `canUpload`, `canYoutube` (`canRequest()` = any of them; old factories kept); `ControlPolicy.Capabilities` gained `canUpload`/`canYoutube` (the WS `you`); new `ControlPolicy.requireSource(actor, via)` checked first in `RoomQueue.add` (guests `403 guest_library_requests_disabled` / `guest_uploads_disabled` / `guest_youtube_disabled`, users `403 forbidden`; no rights at all is left to `cannot_request`). **Guest bans:** `GET /api/rooms/{room}/guest-bans?cursor=&limit=` (newest first), `GET/PUT/DELETE …/{guestId}` (GET also when not banned, so the UI can warn first; PUT 201/200; DELETE 204 / `404 ban_not_found`), owner + moderators (+ moderateAny), users only (`401`), `404 guest_not_found`. A ban is `{guest: actor, identifiedBy, banned, bannedAt?, warning?, ip?}`: `warning` = `RoomGuests.IP_MODE_WARNING` for IP-mode guests (no address in it), `ip` only for `ip.view` via `IpAccess.ipsOfGuests` (the API adds it; the `GuestBan` record has no IP field). Ban/unban run on the room executor: row + audit (`room.guest_banned` / `room.guest_unbanned`, details guestId + kind, no IP) in the room transaction, the guest's connections removed from presence, events `GuestBanned(guestId, connectionIds)` (for the WS layer to kick) / `GuestUnbanned`. `Rooms.access` gives a room-banned guest `403 banned_from_room`, `Rooms.list` hides the room from it. **Decision:** a banned guest's queue entries and votes stay (as for banned users). **Presence:** `RoomPresence` in `RoomState` (connection id → actor key|null + mode `listen`/`remote`); `Counts(listening, remote, anonymous)`: an identified actor counts once (listening if any socket listens, else remote), anonymous counts sockets; `presentIdentified()` is derived from it (the old setter is gone). `PresenceOps.join/leave/mode/identify` (executor): a change of the counts emits `PresenceChanged(counts)` (so a version bump, like every change); a third tab that changes no count emits nothing; fewer identified members → `QueueControl.checkVoteSkip`. In memory only: kept when the executor reloads its state (`adoptPresence`), empty after a restart. Docs: `api.md` guest-bans row, `rooms.md` "as implemented" notes. Tests: `RoomGuestsContract` (7, SQLite + PostgreSQL: capability matrix server × room for each capability, guests disabled / server ban / ytdlp off, room actors, room half via `AddMediaPolicy`, ban lifecycle incl. who may, listing/paging, events/presence/version, audit, cascade on room delete, IP-mode warning without address, presence counting/events/reload, vote-skip on a presence drop), `RoomPresenceTest`, `ControlPolicyTest.requestPaths` + capabilities, `QueueOpsContract` guest paths, `RoomsApiTest.guestBans` (HTTP incl. `ip` only for the admin), `PermissionMatrixTest` rows for the 4 routes, `DtoIpLeakTest` (routes seeded, swept, `ip.view` control hits on guest-bans, new records classified). `mvn verify` passes (856 unit + 288 integration). For the WebSocket step: get the `RoomActor` from `RoomGuests.roomActor`, call `PresenceOps` on join/leave/mode/identity, kick sockets on `GuestBanned`. Owner UI (later step) needs the server switches for greying out: not exposed yet.
- [x] Request scope search (`owner` / `owner+self` / `none`), no library-origin leaks.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `b65d00e`, in `rooms/`. `RequestSearch` + `GET /api/rooms/{room}/search?q=&kind=&cursor=&limit=` (in `RoomsApi`) → `{items:[Library.ItemView], nextCursor?}`. Scope: `owner` = the owner's library ∪ items in the owner's playlists; `owner+self` adds the searching user's library (guests have none); `none` → `403 library_requests_disabled` (except the owner, who still finds their own library; moderators follow the scope). **No origin leaks:** one `SELECT … FROM items WHERE id IN (…) OR id IN (…)`, so each item once, metadata only (no `addedAt`/`via`/`sharedBy`), ordered by item id DESC (item creation, the same for every library; cursor = last id), never by library add time. `q`/`kind` as `/api/library` (`Library.terms` made public). Failed/purged uploads left out; failed/purged YouTube items listed (requesting refetches). Who: anyone `Rooms.access` lets in; guests and anonymous need the effective `guests.canRequestLibrary` (`403 guest_library_requests_disabled`). **Decision:** the scope is also enforced on `queue.add` by `itemId` for non-moderators (`RoomQueue.add` → `RequestSearch.requireInScope`, after `checkAdd`): `403 not_in_request_scope` (same answer for unknown ids, so it reveals no more than the search) / `library_requests_disabled`; owner and moderators add anything. `QueueOpsContract`'s test items now sit in the owner's library. Tests: `RequestSearchContract` (SQLite + PostgreSQL: scopes, who may search, query/kind/pages, no origin fields, by-id scope), `QueueOpsContract.requestsByIdFollowTheRequestScope`, `RoomsApiTest.requestSearch`, a `PermissionMatrixTest` row, `DtoIpLeakTest` (DTO + sweep). Docs: `api.md` search row, `libraries.md` as-implemented note. `mvn verify` passes (865 unit + 296 integration).
- [x] `room_requests` writing; request-history API (owner rw, moderators ro), filters, add-to-library, trim after `rooms.requestHistoryDays`.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `d9bf878`, in `rooms/RoomRequests`. **Writing:** `QueueOps.add(tx, a, itemId, via)` (`RoomQueue.add` passes `Source.via()`) inserts the row in the same transaction as the entry (suggestions too), `by_name` = the user's/guest's `display_name` then, plus `RoomEvent.RequestAdded(requestId, entryId, via)` for the WS `request.new`. **Who is a request (decision, from rooms.md's wording):** every add except by the room's owner, and in `owner` mode except by moderators; `addPlaylist` never; the 3-arg `QueueOps.add` writes none. **API** (in `RoomsApi`): `GET /api/rooms/{room}/requests?source=&requester=&cursor=&limit=` (users only; owner + moderators + moderateAny; newest first by (at, id); `source` library/upload/youtube, `requester` = user or guest id; request = `{id, item: ItemView, requester?: ActorRef with the stored name, requesterName, via, at, entryId?, addedToOwnerLibrary, inOwnerLibrary}`; purged items listed with `status: purged`; a gone requester → no `requester`, name kept). `POST …/requests/add-to-library {requestIds}` (1–100, deduplicated; **owner only**, so moderateAny gets 403) → `{results:[{requestId, itemId?, created?, error?}]}` per id: `Library.add(owner, itemId, VIA_REQUEST, requireYoutube)` (new overload; entries get `via='request'`), row flagged `added_to_owner_library`; per-id errors `request_not_found` (other room / trimmed), `media_expired`, `item_failed`, YouTube refetch refusals. **Trim:** new server setting `rooms.requestHistoryDays` (default 90, 0 = never, max 36500; `Settings.roomsRequestHistoryDays()`); rows past it are hidden from list/add at once, and `RoomRequests.trim()` deletes them, run after every hourly retention sweep via the new `RetentionSweeper.alsoRun(name, chore)` (failures logged per chore). `TestUploads.roomsApi` now takes the `Config`. Docs: `api.md` rows, `auth.md` settings table, `libraries.md` as-implemented note. Tests: `RoomRequestsContract` (5, SQLite + PostgreSQL: who writes rows, three sources, event, list access/filters/pages/purged/gone requester/name at request time, add-to-library results incl. refetch and feature off, transfer, trim + cutoff + 0, cascade), `RoomsApiTest.requestHistory`, two `PermissionMatrixTest` rows, `DtoIpLeakTest` (seeded guest request, sweep, new DTOs/internal records), `RetentionSweeperContract` chores, `SettingsContract` counts. `mvn verify` passes (872 unit + 302 integration).
- [x] Replace the M2 current/next stub with the live `Set<itemId>` maintained by room executors.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `3dec9e2`, in `rooms/`. `LivePlayingItems` (implements `PlayingItems`; owned by `RoomRegistry`, exposed as `registry.playingItems()` and passed to `MediaApi` in `Main` instead of `NO_ROOMS`, which stays for tests): lock-free reads from a `ConcurrentHashMap<itemId, roomCount>`; per-room sets updated under a lock, new items added before old ones are dropped (an item that stays listed is never briefly missing); each room's set is tagged with the executor that published it, so a late-closing executor can't withdraw what its replacement published. `RoomExecutor` publishes `LivePlayingItems.of(state)` after **every** mutation and scheduled task (also failed/read-only ones, so a reload republishes), **before** completing the caller's future (a returning `call` sees the set current), and withdraws it in `closeNow` (delete, shutdown, reload failure). **What counts (decision, doc updated in `libraries.md#media-access-rules`):** the current entry's item (playing or paused), the first queued entry in play order, and the first *ready* queued entry (`Playback.nextEligible`; differs when the first one is still ingesting and would be skipped over). Suggestions don't count. Only **loaded** rooms count: playing rooms are loaded at start, and opening a room (room view, joining) loads it, so a paused room nobody opened since a restart lists nothing until someone does. An item that becomes ready via ingest only changes "first ready" at the room's next mutation (there's still no ingest → rooms notification; see the timeline step's follow-up). Tests: `LivePlayingItemsTest` (3: counts across rooms, owner-checked removal, no gap for a kept item under 100k concurrent updates), `PlaybackContract.theLiveSetFollowsTheCurrentAndNextEntries` (SQLite + PostgreSQL: not loaded → nothing, idle next, current + first + first-ready, paused, skip, advance task, idle with a processing entry, failed mutation republishes the committed state, close withdraws); `DtoIpLeakTest` classifies the new record INTERNAL. `mvn verify` passes (876 unit + 303 integration). Jar start-up checked (anonymous `/media/…/audio.m4a` → 403).
- [x] Retention integration for queue entry states.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `65de0ce` (no schema change). The sweeper's predicate already treated `queued`/`suggested`/`playing` entries as references (M2); this step makes `last_referenced_at` follow them. **Gap fixed:** an entry ending as `played`/`skipped` (`Playback.advance`: natural end, next, vote-skip, resume skipping) didn't touch its item, so an item played once was purged counting from when it was added, not when it stopped playing. **Decision: centralised** in `RoomStore.setEntryState`, which now takes `now` and calls the new `References.touchQueueEntry` (item via the entry, never backwards) after any state change, so no path can be missed: played, skipped, removed, rejected suggestion, and the "gains a reference" cases (accepted suggestion, an entry made current again by `previous`, the requeued current). Touches within the referenced group only extend retention (harmless). The explicit touches in `QueueOps.remove`/`QueueControl.reject` became redundant and were dropped; `insert` keeps its touch. Room delete's inline SQL moved to `References.touchRoomQueue` (References stays the only writer). Not covered (no such path exists yet): guest → account merge (M6) moves entries without changing state. Test: `QueueOpsContract.everyQueueEntryStateChangeTouchesItsItem` (SQLite + PostgreSQL: add, skip, playing, previous, requeue, remove, played; a finished entry isn't touched again). `libraries.md#retention` updated. `mvn verify` passes (877 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] WebSocket protocol (`docs/api.md#websocket`): auth, time, join/leave/mode, sync, identity ticket, all queue/playback messages, `ack`/`error` with `rid`, `identity_required`; full state vs deltas with `version`; `request.new` to owner/mods; unknown types ignored.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `0d7e8f6`, in `rooms/`. `RoomSocket` (`GET /ws`, registered in `Main.router`, which now takes it; actor resolved by a route hook before the upgrade, so a bad bearer header is a plain 401): per socket an `identity` message on connect, a bounded outbox (1024) with one sender thread (overflow → close 1008), messages handled in order on the socket thread except `queue.add`/`queue.addPlaylist` (own virtual thread, max 4 per socket, `429 too_many_requests`) so `time` keeps being answered. `auth` before join only; `identity` redeems `IdentityTickets` (shared with `MeApi`), then `PresenceOps.identify` + fresh state. Every room action re-resolves the socket's session/guest (revoked → `kicked session_ended`, error, close) and builds a fresh `RoomActor` via `RoomGuests.roomActor` (access re-checked). Queue/playback via `RoomQueue` (now wired in `Main`). `ack {rid,...result}` / `error {rid?, status, code, message}`; unknown types ignored. `RoomBroadcaster` (one per `RoomExecutor`, its `RoomListener`): the executor thread only takes an immutable `RoomSnapshot` (skipped while nobody is subscribed); a virtual thread builds per-socket messages with DB lookups (`RoomViews`: item metadata + media URLs, adder names, last 20 history), so the executor never waits. Joins/resends are queued from inside the mutation, so each socket sees its state and then every later change in version order. **Decisions:** per change exactly one message per socket with the new `version`: `presence` (presence-only), `timeline` (re-anchor on the same entry), full `state` up to 200 waiting entries, else a `queue` delta `{upsert, removed, order, suggestionOrder, timeline, current, history, skipVotes, presence}` (diffed against what that socket last got) instead of the per-op sketch; room/settings/member changes always full state. New `RoomEvent.MemberChanged(userId)` from `PUT`/`DELETE …/members/{userId}` (DELETE now also runs on the executor), so **role changes now bump the room version** (RoomsApiTest updated) and the broadcaster re-checks those sockets; `RoomUpdated`/`SettingsChanged` re-check all. `kicked` reasons: `banned_from_room`, `room_not_found` (went private), `room_deleted`, `room_closed` (rejoin), `session_ended`; kicked sockets stay open. `you` gained `identified` and `canModerate`; `ControlPolicy.capabilities(ControlMode, RoomActor)` added. `outputs: []` until M4, `overlay` left for M5. Full shapes in `docs/api.md#as-implemented-m3-roomsocket-roombroadcaster`. Tests: `RoomSocketTest` (7, over a real socket: identity/time/invalid messages/bearer auth, state then consecutive-version deltas incl. timeline/presence/sync/leave/close, guest via ticket + `identity_required` + yourVote + guest-ban kick, `request.new` only to owner, role change/ban/private/delete kicks, revoked session, >200-entry queue deltas; every message checked for the client IP), `PermissionMatrixTest` row for `GET /ws`, `DtoIpLeakTest` (owner + anonymous sockets join and add, all messages scanned; `/ws` swept; new records classified). `mvn verify` passes (884 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] `/r/{slug}` redirect to the room page in remote mode.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `adae452`. `rooms/RoomLinks` (registered in `Main.router`): `GET`/`HEAD /r/{slug}` → `302` with `Location: /rooms/{slug}?mode=remote` and `Cache-Control: no-store`; the slug goes through `Rooms.slug` (lower-cased; not a valid slug, incl. UUID-shaped → `404 room_not_found`, an encoded `/` is the router's `400`). **Decisions:** the room page URL is `/rooms/{idOrSlug}` (like `/items/{id}`), and the mode is passed as `?mode=remote`; `302`, not permanent, since slugs change; the redirect does **not** look the room up, so it never reveals whether a slug exists or is private, and the page's `GET /api/rooms/{room}` applies visibility/bans. Added the `GET /rooms/{room}` route (`Main.ROOM_PAGE` = `room.html`) with a minimal `web/src/room.ts` that loads the room, shows its name and the mode (`modeFromLocation()`: `?mode=remote|listen`, default listen) so the redirect target works now; the room page / remote mode steps below build on it (remembering the mode per room in localStorage is theirs). Tests: `MainRouterTest.shortLinkRedirectsToTheRoomPageInRemoteMode` (302 + Location, case folding, HEAD, invalid slugs 404, POST refused, target page served), `WebBuildTest` (room page packaged, no `requireUser`), `PermissionMatrixTest` rows for both routes (anyone: 200 / 302), `DtoIpLeakTest` sweeps both (a 302 on `/r/` counts as answered). `mvn verify` passes (885 unit + 304 integration).

Web client:

- [x] Room page: now playing, queue, votes, suggestions, request search, upload/YouTube request, history, presence.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `bec22f5`, web only (no server change). `web/src/roomclient.ts`: `RoomConnection` (one socket per page; `join` after every (re)connect, reconnect backoff 0.5/1/2/5/10 s; requests with numeric `rid` matched to `ack`/`error` → `ApiError`, pending ones rejected on close; full `state` replaces, `timeline`/`queue`/`presence` deltas applied only at `version + 1`, a gap sends `sync`, older versions ignored; `kicked` `room_closed` rejoins, other reasons stop; `request.new` passed on; unknown types ignored; `time` replies handed to a hook for the clock-sync step; `positionAt(timeline, serverNow)` freezes unknown states like `paused`). **Identity:** `request()` retries once after `identity_required` via `ensureIdentity()`: `POST /api/me/guest` → (custom names on and name not chosen yet: an inline name form pre-filled with the generated name, no `<dialog>` since Safari 15.0 lacks it; a changed name is sent by a second `POST /api/me/guest {name}`, which renames and returns a fresh ticket) → `{type:"identity", ticket}` (`already_identified` tolerated). `web/src/room.ts` + `room.html`: header (name, mode, control mode, role, presence `N listening · N remote · +N anonymous`), identity line (actor + guest badge, "Change name" for guests with custom names), now playing (cover, title/artist, state, added by, progress; for `canSkip` a seek slider and Previous/Play|Pause/Skip; vote mode "Vote to skip (votes/needed)" for `canVote`, else the count), queue (vote mode: ▲/▼ with `yourVote` pressed, clicking again sends 0, ▼ only with `allowDownvotes`; Pin/Unpin for moderators; owner mode moderators / open mode request rights: ↑/↓ via `queue.move`; Remove for moderators, own entries, open-mode request rights), suggestions (Accept/Reject for moderators, Withdraw for the suggester; the panel is shown in owner mode or while suggestions exist), request panel (library search `GET /api/rooms/{id}/search` with kind filter and More paging, "Add to queue" or "Suggest"; upload with progress → `queue.add {uploadId}`; YouTube link → `queue.add {youtubeUrl}`; owner/moderator users: playlist + shuffle → `queue.addPlaylist`), history (played/skipped, time, added by), `request.new` notices for owner/mods (last 5, dismissable). The position uses the client clock (`clock.offsetMs = 0`) until the clock-sync step. **Decisions:** (1) the `you` capabilities are all false for an anonymous socket (`ControlPolicy.capabilities` needs `identified`), so the page can't tell what a guest could do; instead an anonymous visitor sees "Join as guest" (when `guests.enabled` and the room switches on any `guests.*` capability) or a login link, and the full `state` after the identity shows the real rights. (2) A guest uploads with `roomId` (its rights come from the room); a **user's** upload is sent without `roomId`, because `POST /api/uploads` still answers a user's `roomId` with `404 room_not_found` (M2 `TODO(rooms, M3)` in `UploadsApi.create`, left as is: the user's rights are `library.upload` anyway, and `queue.add {uploadId}` makes it the request); users get an "Also add to my library" checkbox (default off), guests send `addToLibrary` false. `Upload` gained `roomId` and an `uploadId` getter. Tests: `WebBuildTest` (room.js sends every WS action type the page offers, uses `/search?`, `uploadId`, `youtubeUrl`; roomclient.js uses `/ws`, `join`, `sync`, `/api/me/guest`, `identity`, `identity_required`; room.html has the sections). Smoke-tested against the jar with headless Chrome over CDP (scripts not kept): anonymous view of a playing vote room; Join as guest → name form → identity line; vote (count 1, pressed); search + request; own-entry Remove; owner: Pause/Play, Pin/Unpin, switch to owner mode (full state, move arrows), a guest's suggestion → `request.new` notice → Accept → ↑ move → Skip into history; guest upload in open mode → `queue.add {uploadId}` → "Processing" entry. No console errors. `mvn verify` passes (886 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] Clock sync (8 exchanges, min-RTT, 30 s repeat, EMA, outlier filter).
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `30f8d7f`, web only (the server's `time` reply already existed). `web/src/clocksync.ts` (no DOM, no imports; `clientNow()` moved here from `roomclient.ts`): `ClockSync(send)` with `start()` (on every (re)connect), `stop()` (on disconnect; the offset is kept), `reply(t0, ts, t1)`, `syncNow()`, `serverNow()` (the client's `Date.now()` until the first round), `offsetMs`/`rttMs`, `onUpdate`. A **round** = 8 exchanges sent one after the other (the next when the reply arrives, or after a 2 s reply timeout; late replies are ignored), keeping the lowest-RTT sample; a round starts on connect and every 30 s. **Decisions/tuning (doc updated in `rooms.md#clock-sync-web-and-app`):** EMA α = 0.3 over the rounds' best samples, the first one taken as is; outlier filter: a round is ignored when its best RTT is above `max(2 × minRtt, minRtt + 5 ms)` (the slack so sub-ms jitter on a LAN doesn't count), with `minRtt` over the last 10 rounds (about 5 min) so a network that got slower for good is accepted again; a sample more than 1 s from the smoothed offset **replaces** it (the monotonic client clock stepped, e.g. the device slept); the page also runs a round at once when it becomes visible again. The room page's `serverNow()` (progress bar, and later listen mode) uses it; the instance is exposed as `window.togetherTunesClock` for the manual two-browser check. Tests: `src/test/resources/web-tests/clocksync.test.mjs` (11 cases on a simulated clock/network: sequential 8 exchanges and min-RTT pick, 30 s repeat + EMA, outlier rejection, window expiry, LAN slack, step, reply timeout + late reply, no offset yet, disconnect/reconnect, `syncNow`), run with the build's Node (`web/node/node --test`) by `ClockSyncJsTest`, which also checks the room page uses it; mutating the filter or α makes them fail. Smoke-tested against the jar with a Node WebSocket client (8 sent, offset −0.7 ms, RTT 0.2 ms on localhost). `mvn verify` passes (887 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] Listen mode: audio items play their original with `originalGainDb` via `GainNode` (fallback to `audio.m4a` if not web-playable, `canPlayType` says no, or hard seeks don't converge); sync loop (hard seek / rate nudge / steady), `preservesPitch`, preload next ~10 s before end, "Click to listen" autoplay overlay.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `ac0a78f`. Server: item views gained `originalType` (MIME for `canPlayType`, from the kept original's extension: `audio/mpeg`, `audio/flac`, `audio/wav`, `audio/aac`, `audio/mp4; codecs="mp4a.40.2"`, `audio/ogg; codecs="opus"`, `audio/webm; codecs="opus"`; omitted for other extensions). Web: `web/src/listensync.ts` (pure, Node-tested: `syncAction` (>750 ms seek, >40 ms rate `clamp(1 − drift/2000, 0.95, 1.05)`, else 1), `dbToGain`, `chooseSource`, `nextToPreload`/`shouldPreload`, `SeekGuard`) and `web/src/listen.ts` `ListenPlayer`, created by `room.ts` in listen mode only, ticked every 250 ms, on every state and on every clock-sync update; stopped on a final kick. **Decisions:** (1) a fixed pool of 2 `<audio>` + 2 `<video playsinline>` elements reused for all entries (iOS blesses elements per gesture; the overlay click touches all four), each with its own `GainNode` created once (preloaded element at gain 0). (2) Overlay shown while the timeline plays and the `AudioContext` isn't `running` or `play()` hit `NotAllowedError`. (3) Fallback to `audio.m4a` also on an element `error`; the hard-seek guard counts only seeks made while the element was playing with data (a blocked/paused element is moved uncounted; found in the smoke test: before the fix, an autoplay-blocked original fell back after 2 s), reset on re-anchor/reload. (4) Paused timeline: element paused and kept within 100 ms. (5) Video items without `presentAsAudio` play `mezz.mp4` in a visible `<video>` for now (the audio-only toggle/crossfade is the next step). (6) `window.togetherTunesPlayer.debug()` for the manual checks. `docs/rooms.md#listen-mode` (as implemented) and `docs/api.md` updated. Tests: `listensync.test.mjs` (11 cases) via `ListenJsTest` (also checks the player uses `createMediaElementSource`/`createGain`/`preservesPitch`/`canPlayType`/`NotAllowedError` and the page has the overlay), `RoomViewsTest` (originalType), `NodeTests` helper shared with `ClockSyncJsTest`. Smoke-tested against the jar in headless Chrome over CDP (default autoplay policy): overlay shown, click → context running, FLAC original played with its gain (+27.75 dB on a −20 dB tone), drift steady at −1…−10 ms, owner seek → hard seek to within 5 ms, pause → both elements paused, paused seek → element at 27 000 ms, play resumes in sync, next entry preloaded with ≤10 s left and taken over at drift −5 ms, a video item plays `mezz.mp4` (1280 px, drift −32 ms). Not checked here (needs real devices; covered by the M3 manual checks): Safari/iOS 15 autoplay unlock, `GainNode` on iOS, `canPlayType` fallback on a browser without FLAC/Opus, VBR-MP3 seek fallback in practice. Note for iOS: Web Audio output follows the ringer/silent switch on iOS 15. `mvn verify` passes (892 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] Video ↔ audio-only crossfade switch (two synced elements, 150 ms ramp via Web Audio `GainNode`, stop video download in audio-only).
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `34f701a`, web only. `listensync.ts`: `chooseSource(…, audioOnly)` (video items without `presentAsAudio` → `audio.m4a` in audio-only mode, so `mezz.mp4` is never requested; audio items unchanged) and `SwitchGate` (crossfade once the incoming element's drift was ≤ 40 ms on 3 consecutive ticks counted at least 200 ms apart, since the loop also ticks on bursts of state/load events; or 3 s after the switch began, but only while the incoming element is playing with data, so a slow or failing load never leaves a gap); `CROSSFADE_MS = 150`. `listen.ts`: `ListenPlayer(…, audioOnly)`, `setAudioOnly()`/`audioOnly`; a switch keeps the outgoing deck at full gain and synced while the incoming one (from the same 2+2 pool) loads and syncs at gain 0, then `linearRampToValueAtTime` on both `GainNode`s (stepped `volume` without Web Audio), then the outgoing element is emptied (`removeAttribute("src")` + `load()`, stops the download) right after the ramp. **Decisions:** (1) the toggle is an "Audio only" checkbox above the player, shown in listen mode, remembered per browser for all rooms (`localStorage` `togethertunes.audioOnly`; mode-per-room comes with remote mode). (2) Paused timeline, autoplay blocked / context not running, or the outgoing element not playing: switch at once (nothing audible to fade). (3) Toggling back mid-switch swaps roles (the still-loaded element becomes the incoming one). (4) The video stays in view until its fade-out starts; an incoming video is shown when its fade-in starts. (5) In audio-only mode the now-playing panel's cover art stands in for the video (the player area is hidden, as for audio items). (6) Fix found on the way: `.room-player video { display: block }` overrode `hidden`, so the preloaded video was visible too; added `.room-player video[hidden] { display: none }`. `debug()` shows `audioOnly` and the switch (`from`, `to`, `fading`, `toDriftMs`). Tests: 4 new cases in `listensync.test.mjs` (audio-only source, gate counting/reset/timeout); `ListenJsTest` also checks `linearRampToValueAtTime`, `SwitchGate`, the toggle. Smoke-tested against the jar in headless Chrome over CDP: video → audio-only: `audio.m4a` synced within ~60 ms (drift 0 ms), fade started at +660 ms, `mezz.mp4` element emptied and hidden at +830 ms, audio drift 0…−3 ms after; audio-only → video: video synced at −20 ms, fade at +710 ms, shown from the fade, `audio.m4a` emptied at +880 ms; reload with audio-only stored: checkbox restored, only `audio.m4a` and `cover.jpg` requested. Not checked here (M3 manual checks): audible gaps by ear in Chrome/Firefox/iOS Safari, and whether iOS 15 lets the `<video>` and `<audio>` play at the same time (if it pauses one, the switch would cut instead of fade). `mvn verify` passes (892 unit + 304 integration).
- [x] Remote mode: no media loaded, delayed progress bar (`estimatedDelayMs`, ±0.5 s user nudge in localStorage); mode remembered per room.
  - Done 2026-11-09 at main `b819668`, web only (the server's `mode` message and the `estimatedDelayMs.*` room settings already existed). `web/src/remote.ts` (no DOM, Node-tested; `RoomMode` moved here, re-exported by `roomclient.ts`): `chooseMode` (URL `?mode=` wins, then the room's remembered mode, then listen), `outputDelay`, `stepNudge`/`parseNudge`/`effectiveDelay`, `DelayedTimeline`. `room.ts`: a header toggle ("I'm listening in VRChat (remote mode)" / "Listen here instead") sends `{type:"mode"}`, updates `conn.mode` for reconnects, stores `togethertunes.mode.<roomId>` and strips `?mode=` from the address so a reload keeps the choice; remote mode creates no `ListenPlayer` (no `AudioContext`, no media elements), and switching listen → remote stops and empties the player's elements (the player is reused when switching back, unlocked by the toggle click). Remote controls: "Delay 6.0 s (RTMP 5.0 s, your nudge +1.0 s)" with −0.5 s / +0.5 s / Reset, stored per room as `togethertunes.remoteNudge.<roomId>`. **Decisions:** (1) the **active output** is the most preferred of RTMP > `.ts` > HLS in `state.outputs` that isn't `error`/`disabled`/`stopped`/`off`; delay = the output's own `estimatedDelayMs`, else the room setting, else the default; with no outputs listed (all of M3) RTMP (5 s) is assumed. M4's room-UI step (`output.status`, `estimatedDelayMs` defaults) may refine this once outputs carry real statuses. (2) Not just the bar: the whole now-playing panel (entry, state, position) shows the timeline as it was `delay` ago, so a skip appears when it's heard in VRChat. Each timeline change is dated at its `anchorServerMs` if plausible (≤ 10 s before arrival, not in the future), else at arrival; the state on joining counts as always in effect; snapshots older than 150 s are dropped. Controls (skip/pause/seek/votes) act on the live room. (3) Nudge per room (outputs and their lag differ between rooms), whole 0.5 s steps, total delay kept within 0–120 s. (4) Cover art is still loaded in remote mode ("no media" = no audio/video). Tests: `remote.test.mjs` (10 cases) via `RemoteJsTest` (also checks the page uses the module, sends `mode`, stops the player, and the controls exist). Note: `WebBuildTest`'s Safari 15.0 check flags any `.at(` call, so the snapshot lookup is named `atTime`. Smoke-tested against the jar in headless Chrome over CDP: `/r/smoke-room` → remote mode, delay 5.0 s (RTMP), no media elements and only `cover.jpg` requested; +0.5 s twice → 6.0 s, stored; owner skip → the panel kept showing the old item until ~6–7 s later, then the new one at 0:01; toggle to listen → player created, `original` requested, `?mode=remote` removed, `mode=listen` stored; reload → listen; toggle back → elements emptied (`src` null); reload → remote, no media elements. `docs/rooms.md#remote-mode` (as implemented) updated. `mvn verify` passes (894 unit + 304 integration).

Results: Brave on the MacBook Pro and Quetta (Chromium) on a Pixel 7 Pro stayed in sync flawlessly. **Every Safari** (Mac Safari, iPad Pro M1 on iPadOS 26.1, iPhone X on iOS 15.1) stuttered while playing video, and sometimes played a second or two late at a slightly higher speed *and higher pitch*. **iPhone X (iOS 15.1)** didn't sync at all: it played from the beginning every time the page was reloaded. Collect the same ring in memory always, so the report also covers what happened before the click.

Fix what you find, and make the sync loop never die silently (catch, record in the diagnostics ring, keep ticking).
  3. **Safari stutter / late + fast + high pitch:** likely causes to address: (a) Safari stalls the video on every `playbackRate` change, and the loop sets a slightly different rate almost every tick: quantise the nudge (e.g. one step of ±3 %, or very few steps) with hysteresis so the rate changes rarely; (b) a hard seek in Safari takes hundreds of ms (decoding from the 2 s GOP's keyframe), so the element lands late and then rate-nudges for seconds: measure seek latency per element and seek ahead by it (target + expected latency), and re-check after `seeked`; (c) pitch rises with the rate, so `preservesPitch` / `webkitPreservesPitch` isn't honoured, possibly because the element is routed through `createMediaElementSource`: if the pitch can't be kept in Safari, don't rate-nudge there at all: correct only with (latency-compensated) seeks above a larger threshold, or another approach you can justify. Keep Chrome/Firefox behaviour unchanged unless the change is clearly better for them too.

- [x] Safari seek-mode follow-up (added 2026-11-10, from Siggi's retest at main `94434e3`; diagnostics `s0otvo` = iPhone X iOS 15.1, `n15p1p` = iPad Pro iPadOS 26.1, which reports a *Macintosh* UA; both lines are in the 8080 server's `server.log`, find them with `grep '"ref":"s0otvo"'`). **Good:** the iPhone X now syncs (no more start from 0), no page errors, both in `seek` mode with rate 1. **Still wrong:**
  1. **The seek lead measures the wrong moment.** In both reports every correction seek "lands" within a few ms at the `seeked` event (iPad: `driftMs` 1…6, `tookMs` 73–103, lead learned ≈ 42 ms), but in the next ~600 ms the drift falls to about **−300 ms on the iPad** and about **−280 ms on the iPhone** and then stays constant (iPad −305…−338 after every seek at 29.6, 32.1, 34.6 … 44.6 s; iPhone −130 after `seeked` at 148). So WebKit keeps the media clock still for ~300 ms *after* `seeked` (pipeline refill) and then plays at normal speed. Fix: learn the lead from the **settled** drift (e.g. the median drift 0.8–1.5 s after `seeked`, once it stops changing), not from the drift at `seeked`; seek ahead by that, so one or two seeks converge instead of seeking every 2.5 s with the same −300 ms result. Keep the existing fast re-seek for a seek that lands far off, but judge it on the settled value too.
  2. **Backoff parks the player off-sync.** After a few "missed" seeks the cooldown went to 30 s (`seekCooldownMs: 30000` in both), and the iPad then sat at a constant **−210 ms** and the iPhone at **−104 ms** for the rest of the report (iPad 47–75 s, iPhone 56–81 s), well outside the 60 ms band. A *systematic* miss (same settled offset every time) should feed the lead, not the backoff; back off only when seeks scatter (settled drift not repeatable) and cap the cooldown much lower (e.g. 5–8 s).
  3. **Each seek is audible** on WebKit (~300 ms stall), so seek mode must seek rarely: after the lead has converged, the steady drift should be constant (the reports show rate-1 playback holds the offset to ±1 ms for 30 s), so one good seek should hold for minutes. Consider a slightly larger seek threshold once converged (e.g. 80–100 ms) if that avoids repeated corrections; justify the number from the reports.
  Also: the iPad reports `Macintosh` with `maxTouchPoints: 5`; make sure nothing keys off the platform string. `stalled` events in the reports are network idle (readyState 4) and harmless. Extend `listensync.test.mjs` with a simulated WebKit element (clock frozen ~300 ms after `seeked`) and check convergence in ≤ 2 seeks and no 30 s parking. Smoke-test as before (headless Chrome with that behaviour emulated). Siggi's own server is on port 8080 (PID 44757, from a jar copy in the scratchpad, with `./data`): leave it alone; use another port and a temporary data dir. Afterwards update the retest note under the 50 ms manual step.
  - Done 2026-11-10 at main `cc039be` (`listensync.ts`: `SeekLanding`, `DriftController`, `SeekLead.guess`; `listen.ts`). 1: a landing is judged on the settled drift (samples from 0.8 s after `seeked`, ≥ 200 ms apart; the median once the last three lie within 20 ms, or the median anyway 3 s after `seeked`); no correction while it settles except a hard seek; the lead learns only from seeks into buffered data (`el.buffered`), an unbuffered one (start, owner seek, the iPhone's 1.4 s seek) is only a first guess before the first real sample. Fast re-seek (> 150 ms) unchanged but on the settled value. 2: cooldown doubles only while seeks scatter (not at rest, or the last three delays = lead used − settled drift spread > 100 ms), cap 8 s (was 30 s). 3: after a seek settles within ±60 ms the threshold is 80 ms: the reports' seven identical correction seeks settled within 33 ms (iPad) / 39 ms (iPhone), so a seek is good to ~±20 ms and can't reliably improve a drift below ~2× that; rate-1 playback held ±1 ms for 30 s. Nothing keys off the platform/UA string (`syncModeFor` uses features; `platform` only appears in the diagnostics payload and `deviceName` on the account page). `listensync.test.mjs`: simulated WebKit element (clock still 280–330 ms after `seeked`, ±17 ms jitter) converges in ≤ 2 seeks from the reports' offsets and stays within ±60 ms for 3 min; systematic misses keep the 2.5 s cooldown; scattering seeks never back off past 8 s; 10 min at 50 ppm needs no further seek. Smoke (headless Chrome, port 18091, scratch data dir, `?sync=seek`, a script holding `currentTime` still 300 ms after every `seeked`): start converged in 2 seeks (hard + 1, lead 314 ms) to ±11 ms; owner seek and a 300 ms push each 1 seek, to ±7 / ±4 ms; 60 s steady with no seek; rate mode (no emulation) unchanged (±13 ms, one ±5 % nudge for the push). `mvn verify`: 913 unit, 304 integration.

Fixes: a seek pending 5 s now **reloads** the element (`load()`, then the normal start seek + `play()`) instead of seeking again; the unlock gesture doesn't seek an element whose seek is still pending (it only calls `play()`; the loop corrects the drift once it plays). **End of an item:** there was a real loop: a seek aimed past the end (target + lead, e.g. a correction 600 ms before the end with an 800 ms lead) was clamped to the last 50 ms, ended the element, and the paused element was then sought back and `play()`ed on every tick. Now no seek and no start once target + lead is within the last 1 s (`END_GUARD_MS`, `inEndGuard`); the element plays out.

- [x] Rooms react when a queued item finishes ingesting (added 2026-11-10, bug found by Siggi): with an empty queue and nothing playing, Siggi uploaded a file into the queue; when ingest finished, the entry's status on the room page stayed "processing" and playback didn't start until another item was added. `docs/rooms.md` says a not-ready entry "becomes eligible as soon as its item is ready", but nothing tells the rooms: ingest (`ingest/`) has no hook into `rooms/` (`RoomStore` only re-reads item status when the executor runs for another reason). Fix: when an item's status changes (ready, failed, purged; also a re-ingest), notify every room that has it queued/current/suggested (look up affected rooms, then on each room's executor): refresh the entry's state and broadcast it to clients (the usual `version` bump / delta), and if the room is idle (or was waiting on a not-ready entry) start the next eligible entry as the timeline rules say (an idle room with an empty queue that gets an entry starts playing it, the same as adding an already-ready item). A failed ingest should show as failed and be skipped. Also check the YouTube request path and the "Loading…"/`LivePlayingItems` current/next set. Tests: a `RoomSocketTest`-style end-to-end case (idle room, queue an upload that is still ingesting, finish the ingest → clients get the state change and playback starts; a second case with something already playing → the entry just turns ready), and one for a failed ingest. Same conventions as before (Siggi's server on 8080 stays untouched).
  - Done 2026-11-10 at main `72dea8f`. `ingest.ItemStatusListener` (`itemStatusChanged(itemId)`): `IngestJobs` calls it for every item status it writes (pending on enqueue, also a refetch from the YouTube request path; processing on claim; ready/failed on finish; startup recovery), `Items.purge` for `purged`, always after the commit through the new `Database.afterCommit` (runs once the outermost transaction committed and its connection is back in the pool, dropped on rollback; `enqueue` joins callers' transactions). `RoomRegistry` is the listener (wired in `Main` after the registry exists): on a virtual thread it finds the rooms with a queued/suggested/playing entry for the item (loading rooms that aren't loaded, so an idle room after a restart starts too) and submits `Playback.itemsChanged` to each: re-reads the items, adds `RoomEvent.EntryItemsChanged` for the entries that changed (so the usual version bump and full state/`queue` delta carry the new `status`), skips a current entry whose item became failed/purged, else `startIfIdle`. A playing room just shows the entry ready; it is picked up at the next advance. Failed entries stay in the queue marked failed and are skipped over. `LivePlayingItems` needed no change: it is republished after every mutation, so the ready entry becomes "next" right away. Extra: at startup `RoomRegistry.startIdleWithReadyEntries()` starts idle rooms that have a ready queued entry (ingests that finished while nothing listened, and rooms already stuck from this bug). Tests in `RoomSocketTest`: idle room + upload still ingesting → finish → one `state` (version+1) with the entry current and playing, advance task set; something playing → the entry turns ready, timeline unchanged; failed ingest → entry `failed`, room stays idle, the next added item plays and the failed one is skipped; an unloaded idle room starts; the startup catch-up. `DatabaseContract` covers `afterCommit`. `mvn verify` green (920 unit, 305 IT).

But on the **iPod touch 7 (iOS 15.1)** the sound **stuttered** and **after a while went mute**, report `dfh3l7` (8080 `server.log`, `grep '"ref":"dfh3l7"'`; video item `justfortonight`, portrait 1080×1920 source, 720p mezzanine). In that report the sync side is perfect: drift 0 ±1 ms from 9.9 s to 47 s, no seeks, `context: "running"`, no errors, no `statechange` events, so the stutter isn't from seeking. Suspects: iOS 15 WebKit's `createMediaElementSource` path (known to crackle, and to go silent after `src` changes/reloads or interruptions; this report had a `reload` at 5.7 s of an element already routed through Web Audio), decoder load on the A10 (720p video + Web Audio), or the AudioContext being interrupted.
  1. **Measure first (diagnostics):** add to the ring/report, sampled every ~1 s: `getVideoPlaybackQuality()` (total/dropped/corrupted frames), `webkitAudioDecodedByteCount` / `webkitVideoDecodedByteCount` / `webkitDecodedFrameCount` / `webkitDroppedFrameCount` where present, `AudioContext.currentTime` progress vs wall clock (a stalled or slow audio clock means glitches), `baseLatency`/`outputLatency`, `ctx.state` (incl. WebKit's `"interrupted"`), and the **output level** of each deck via an `AnalyserNode` after the gain (RMS / peak per second), so "went mute" is visible as level 0 while the element still advances. Also record `pagehide`/`visibilitychange`, audio session interruptions, and `src`/reload events per element. Keep enough history (e.g. 5 minutes at 1 Hz in a compact form) so a report sent a minute after the mute still covers it.
  2. **A/B switch:** `?webaudio=0` on the room URL plays straight from the element without `createMediaElementSource` (gain via `el.volume` where allowed; on iOS volume is read-only, so no gain/crossfade, which is fine for a test), shown in `debug()` and the report, so Siggi can tell whether the Web Audio path causes it.
  3. **Fix what the data or known WebKit bugs point to**, without UA sniffing, e.g.: never change `src` / reload an element that is connected to a `MediaElementAudioSourceNode` on WebKit (use a fresh element from the pool instead, or recreate the source node), resume the context on `"interrupted"`/`suspended` with a user gesture prompt if needed, or fall back to the no-Web-Audio path on engines where it proves unreliable (feature/runtime detection, e.g. output level 0 while the element advances and is not muted → switch path and record it). Research known WebKit bugs (bugs.webkit.org) for iOS 15 `MediaElementAudioSourceNode` with `<video>`.
  Tests for the new logic (Node, as `listensync.test.mjs`), smoke test in headless Chrome. Then write retest steps under the 50 ms manual step: play on the iPod touch for 5+ minutes with and without `?webaudio=0`, and send diagnostics right after a stutter or mute. Siggi's server on 8080 (from a jar copy in the scratchpad, with `./data`) stays untouched.
  - **Done (2026-11-10, main `a78e5a8`).** `mvn verify` passes (922 unit, 305 integration). Cause, by research (no new device data yet): WebKit bug 221334 ("Audio passed through WebAudio is delayed and glitchy on Safari", open since 2021, P2/blocker): on iOS the `MediaElementAudioSourceNode` path stutters, the gaps widen and the sound finally goes completely silent, reported on iPhone 7/8 (A10/A11, the iPod touch 7 has an A10); suspected in `AudioSourceProviderAVFObjC`; no workaround except not using that path. Bug 231191 (crackle on older iOS 15 devices) is related. No known fix keeps the Web Audio path, so:
    - **Diagnostics:** new `web/src/audiohealth.ts`; each element's gain feeds an `AnalyserNode` read every 40 ms; a 300-sample health ring (1 Hz, 5 min) records per element `out: [peak dB, RMS dB, windows, gaps, silent windows]`, `ctx` state, `ck` (audio-clock ms per wall second), position/drift/readyState/gain, `wk` (WebKit decoded audio/video bytes, frames, dropped frames per second), `vq` (playback-quality totals) and `ms` (seconds of silence counted). Events add `visibilitychange`/`pagehide`/`pageshow`, `clear`/`handoff`/`restore`/`resume`/`webaudio-mute`; `debug()` adds `webAudio`, `webAudioOff`, `needsRestore`, `audio: {sampleRate, baseLatencyMs, outputLatencyMs}`; the report adds `webAudioOverride` and trims health after decisions if too big (a full ring is ~40 KB).

    - **Fix (runtime detection, no UA sniffing):** an element that should be heard but whose meter showed only exact zeros for 10 s, after having been heard, trips a mute: the overlay says **"Tap to restore sound"**; the tap rebuilds the pool without Web Audio inside the gesture (`webAudioOff: "mute"`). A stuck seek on a routed element now hands the entry to the other element of its kind instead of `load()` on it (the dfh3l7 reload). A context that was running and turns `suspended`/`interrupted` is resumed (and on `visibilitychange` to visible). Not done: making WebKit default to the element path, because without Web Audio iOS has no gain (no `originalGainDb`, no audio-only crossfade); decide that from the A/B retest. Stutter itself is only measured (gaps, `ck`, dropped frames), not acted on.
